Possible partitions: {3,6} and {12,13}. Evaluate possible outcomes from the pair {3,6}: They can yield 3+6=9, 3-6=-3, 6-3=3, 3*6=18, 6/3=2, 3/6=0.5. And from {12,13}: 12+13=25, 12-13=-1, 13-12=1, 12*13=156, 13/12≈1.0833, 12/13≈0.9231.
Now we want to combine one result from the first pair with one result from the second pair to get 24. Possibilities:
- If we take 18 (from 3*6) and 25 (from 12+13) then 18+25=43, 18-25=-7, 18*25=450, 18/25=0.72, 25-18=7, 25/18≈1.3889, 18/1=18, none equals 24.
- If we take 18 and 1 (from 13-12) then 18+1=19, 18-1=17, 18*1=18, 18/1=18, 1-18=-17, 1/18≈0.0556.
- If we take 18 and -1 (from 12-13) then 18+(-1)=17, 18-(-1)=19, 18*(-1)=-18, 18/(-1)=-18, (-1)-18=-19, (-1)/18≈ -0.0556.
- If we take 18 and (13/12) ≈1.0833, then 18+1.0833=19.0833, 18-1.0833=16.9167, 18*1.0833=19.5, 18/1.0833=16.615, 1.0833-18=-16.9167, 1.0833/18=0.0602.
- If we take 18 and (12/13) ≈0.9231, then 18+0.9231=18.9231, 18-0.9231=17.0769, 18*0.9231=16.6158, 18/0.9231=19.5, 0.9231-18=-17.0769, 0.9231/18=0.0513.

- If we take 2 (from 6/3) and 25 (from 12+13) then 2+25=27, 2-25=-23, 2*25=50, 2/25=0.08, 25-2=23, 25/2=12.5.
- If we take 2 and 1 (from 13-12) then 2+1=3, 2-1=1, 2*1=2, 2/1=2, 1-2=-1, 1/2=0.5.
- If we take 2 and -1 then 2+(-1)=1, 2-(-1)=3, 2*(-1)=-2, 2/(-1)=-2, (-1)-2=-3, (-1)/2=-0.5.
- If we take 2 and (13/12) then 2+13/12 = 2 + 1.0833 = 3.0833, 2-13/12 = 2 - 1.0833 = 0.9167, 2*(13/12)=26/12=2.1667, 2/(13/12)=24/13≈1.846, 13/12 -2 = 1.0833-2=-0.9167, (13/12)/2=13/24≈0.5417.
- If we take 2 and (12/13) then 2+12/13 ≈ 2 + 0.9231 = 2.9231, 2-12/13 ≈ 1.0769, 2*(12/13)=24/13≈1.846, 2/(12/13)=26/12=2.1667, 12/13-2 ≈ -1.0769, (12/13)/2 ≈0.4615.

- If we take 3 (from 6-3) and 25 (from 12+13) then 3+25=28, 3-25=-22, 3*25=75, 3/25=0.12, 25-3=22, 25/3≈8.33.
- If we take 3 and 1 then 3+1=4, 3-1=2, 3*1=3, 3/1=3, 1-3=-2, 1/3≈0.333.
- If we take 3 and -1 then 3+(-1)=2, 3-(-1)=4, 3*(-1)=-3, 3/(-1)=-3, -1-3=-4, -1/3≈-0.333.
- If we take 3 and (13/12) then 3+13/12 = 3 + 1.0833 = 4.0833, 3-13/12 = 3 - 1.0833 = 1.9167, 3*(13/12)=39/12=3.25, 3/(13/12)=36/13≈2.769, 13/12-3=1.0833-3=-1.9167, (13/12)/3=13/36≈0.3611.

I'll now produce final answer.</think>We can notice that if we can “create” a 2 from two of the numbers and then use it to adjust a product of 13 by 2, we might get 24. In fact, observe that

  13 × 2 = 26  and  26 − 2 = 24.

Now, we have the numbers 3, 6, 12, 13. Notice that
  6 ÷ 3 = 2  and  12 ÷ 6 = 2.

So one way to get 2 is to use 6 and 3 in the fraction 6⁄3. Then we can “double” 13 by multiplying by 2 and subtract the other 2 (which we get from 12 ÷ 6). In other words, we write

  13 × (6⁄3) − (12⁄6).

Let’s check:
  6⁄3 = 2  so  13 × 2 = 26,
  12⁄6 = 2,
  and  26 − 2 = 24.

This expression uses each of the four numbers exactly once and only the allowed operations.

Thus, the final answer is

\boxed{13\cdot\frac{6}{3}-\frac{12}{6}}
